# Credits: CompleteAiTraining.com

2,554 TemplatesGrokBot templates are built on lessons from [CompleteAiTraining.com](https://completeaitraining.com), TemplatesGrokBot's sister site for AI training by job (both are part of Nexibeo). The TemplatesGrokBot team turned each lesson's tasks and example prompts into a Grok Bot template, or folded them into an existing template that already did that work.
